1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any arima module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 967. The projective resolution has length at most 552 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_967(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 967. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^552(S, F) is trivial. QED.
